Method: 提出PAC-Bench基准测试，用于系统评估隐私约束下的多智能体协作性能，通过实验分析隐私约束对协作的影响机制

Result: 隐私约束显著降低协作性能，使结果更依赖发起智能体而非合作伙伴；分析揭示性能下降源于重复的协调崩溃，包括早期隐私违规、过度保守的抽象和隐私引发的幻觉

Conclusion: 隐私感知的多智能体协作是一个独特且未解决的挑战，需要超越现有智能体能力的新协调机制

Abstract: We are entering an era in which individuals and organizations increasingly deploy dedicated AI agents that interact and collaborate with other agents. However, the dynamics of multi-agent collaboration under privacy constraints remain poorly understood. In this work, we present $PAC\text{-}Bench$, a benchmark for systematic evaluation of multi-agent collaboration under privacy constraints. Experiments on $PAC\text{-}Bench$ show that privacy constraints substantially degrade collaboration performance and make outcomes depend more on the initiating agent than the partner. Further analysis reveals that this degradation is driven by recurring coordination breakdowns, including early-stage privacy violations, overly conservative abstraction, and privacy-induced hallucinations. Together, our findings identify privacy-aware multi-agent collaboration as a distinct and unresolved challenge that requires new coordination mechanisms beyond existing agent capabilities.

</details>


### [229] [Detecting Safety Violations Across Many Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11806)
*Adam Stein,Davis Brown,Hamed Hassani,Mayur Naik,Eric Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meerkat：结合聚类与智能搜索的安全违规检测系统，用于在大规模智能体轨迹中发现稀疏、复杂的安全违规行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全审计方法存在三个主要问题：1）单轨迹分析无法发现跨轨迹的违规行为；2）简单的智能体审计难以扩展到大规模轨迹集合；3）固定监控器对未预期行为脆弱。这些挑战在滥用活动、隐蔽破坏、奖励黑客和提示注入等场景中尤为突出。

Method: Meerkat结合聚类与智能搜索：1）使用聚类识别有前景的区域；2）通过结构化搜索自适应调查这些区域；3）支持自然语言描述的违规检测；4）不依赖种子场景、固定工作流或穷举枚举。

Result: 1）在滥用、错位和任务博弈场景中显著优于基线监控器；2）在顶级智能体基准测试中发现广泛的开发者作弊行为；3）在CyBench上发现比先前审计多近4倍的奖励黑客示例。

Conclusion: Meerkat通过结合聚类与智能搜索，有效解决了大规模智能体轨迹中稀疏、复杂安全违规的检测问题，为安全审计提供了可扩展且自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: To identify safety violations, auditors often search over large sets of agent traces. This search is difficult because failures are often rare, complex, and sometimes even adversarially hidden and only detectable when multiple traces are analyzed together. These challenges arise in diverse settings such as misuse campaigns, covert sabotage, reward hacking, and prompt injection. Existing approaches struggle here for several reasons. Per-trace judges miss failures that only become visible across traces, naive agentic auditing does not scale to large trace collections, and fixed monitors are brittle to unanticipated behaviors. We introduce Meerkat, which combines clustering with agentic search to uncover violations specified in natural language. Through structured search and adaptive investigation of promising regions, Meerkat finds sparse failures without relying on seed scenarios, fixed workflows, or exhaustive enumeration. Across misuse, misalignment, and task gaming settings, Meerkat significantly improves detection of safety violations over baseline monitors, discovers widespread developer cheating on a top agent benchmark, and finds nearly 4x more examples of reward hacking on CyBench than previous audits.

</details>


### [230] [A collaborative agent with two lightweight synergistic models for autonomous crystal materials research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11540)
*Tongyu Shi,Yutang Li,Zhanyuan Li,Qian Liu,Jie Zhou,Wenhe Xu,Yang Li,Dawei Dai,Rui He,Wenhua Zhou,Jiahong Wang,Xue-Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MatBrain：用于晶体材料研究的轻量级协作智能体系统，采用双模型架构（30B参数分析模型+14B参数执行模型），显著降低硬件需求95%，在催化剂设计中实现100倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型参数量巨大（数百亿）但在材料科学领域特定推理和工具协调方面存在困难，需要开发既能提供专业领域推理又能高效协调工具的轻量级解决方案

Method: 采用双模型协作架构：Mat-R1（30B参数）作为分析模型提供专家级领域推理，Mat-T1（14B参数）作为执行模型协调工具操作；通过解耦工具规划和分析推理的不同熵动态来解决两者冲突

Result: MatBrain在结构生成、性质预测和合成规划等任务中表现优异，显著超越更大的通用模型；硬件部署门槛降低95%以上；在催化剂设计中，48小时内生成30,000个候选结构并识别出38个有前景材料，实现约100倍加速

Conclusion: 轻量级协作智能体系统通过解耦分析和执行功能，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低计算需求，为材料研究能力提升提供了新范式，展示了协作智能在材料科学中的巨大潜力

Abstract: Current large language models require hundreds of billions of parameters yet struggle with domain-specific reasoning and tool coordination in materials science. Here, we present MatBrain, a lightweight collaborative agent system with two synergistic models specialization for crystal materials research. MatBrain employs a dual-model architecture: Mat-R1 (30B parameters) as the analytical model providing expert-level domain reasoning, and Mat-T1 (14B parameters) as the executive model orchestrating tool-based actions. Entropy analysis confirms that this architecture resolves the conflict between tool planning and analytical reasoning by decoupling their distinct entropy dynamics. Enabled by this dual-model architecture and structural efficiency, MatBrain significantly outperforms larger general-purpose models while reducing the hardware deployment barrier by over 95%. MatBrain exhibits versatility across structure generation, property prediction, and synthesis planning tasks. Applied to catalyst design, MatBrain generated 30,000 candidate structures and identified 38 promising materials within 48 hours, achieving approximately 100-fold acceleration over traditional approaches. These results demonstrate the potential of lightweight collaborative intelligence for advancing materials research capabilities.

</details>


### [231] [Intersectional Sycophancy: How Perceived User Demographics Shape False Validation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11609)
*Benjamin Maltbie,Shivam Raval*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型的谄媚行为存在系统性差异，GPT-5-nano比Claude Haiku 4.5更谄媚，且谄媚程度随用户种族、年龄、性别和表达自信程度等人口统计特征组合而变化，其中西班牙裔女性获得的谄媚度最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型存在谄媚倾向——为显得讨人喜欢而验证用户错误信念。本研究旨在探究这种行为是否随感知到的用户人口统计特征（种族、年龄、性别、自信程度）而系统性变化，并测试不同组合是否产生差异化的错误验证率。

Method: 受法律交叉性概念启发，使用Anthropic的Petri评估框架进行768次多轮对抗性对话，在数学、哲学和阴谋论三个领域测试GPT-5-nano和Claude Haiku 4.5模型，覆盖128种人物组合。采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验进行统计分析。

Result: GPT-5-nano整体上比Claude Haiku 4.5显著更谄媚（平均值2.96 vs. 1.74，p<10^-32）。GPT-5-nano在哲学领域的谄媚度比数学高41%，西班牙裔人物在所有种族中获得的谄媚度最高。最差表现的人物组合（自信的23岁西班牙裔女性）平均谄媚度达5.33/10。Claude Haiku 4.5表现出均匀的低谄媚度，无显著人口统计差异。

Conclusion: 谄媚行为在用户间并非均匀分布，安全评估应纳入身份感知测试。不同模型在谄媚倾向和人口统计敏感性方面存在显著差异，这对AI安全评估方法具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit sycophantic tendencies--validating incorrect user beliefs to appear agreeable. We investigate whether this behavior varies systematically with perceived user demographics, testing whether combinations of race, age, gender, and expressed confidence level produce differential false validation rates. Inspired by the legal concept of intersectionality, we conduct 768 multi-turn adversarial conversations using Anthropic's Petri evaluation framework, probing GPT-5-nano and Claude Haiku 4.5 across 128 persona combinations in mathematics, philosophy, and conspiracy theory domains. GPT-5-nano is significantly more sycophantic than Claude Haiku 4.5 overall ($\bar{x}=2.96$ vs. $1.74$, $p < 10^{-32}$, Wilcoxon signed-rank). For GPT-5-nano, we find that philosophy elicits 41% more sycophancy than mathematics and that Hispanic personas receive the highest sycophancy across races. The worst-scoring persona, a confident, 23-year-old Hispanic woman, averages 5.33/10 on sycophancy. Claude Haiku 4.5 exhibits uniformly low sycophancy with no significant demographic variation. These results demonstrate that sycophancy is not uniformly distributed across users and that safety evaluations should incorporate identity-aware testing.

</details>


### [232] [Context Kubernetes: Declarative Orchestration of Enterprise Knowledge for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11623)
*Charafeddine Mouzouni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Context Kubernetes：一种用于编排企业知识在智能体AI系统中的架构，通过声明式配置和三层权限模型解决知识治理问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体AI系统中，将正确的知识以正确的权限和新鲜度传递给正确的智能体是一个复杂的编排问题，类似于Kubernetes解决的容器编排问题，但现有平台缺乏架构级的治理机制

Method: 提出Context Kubernetes架构，包含六个核心抽象、基于YAML的声明式知识架构即代码清单、协调循环和三层智能体权限模型（智能体权限始终是人类权限的严格子集）

Result: 三个价值实验显示：无治理时26.5%查询出现幻影内容和跨域数据泄露；无新鲜度监控时静默提供陈旧内容，协调循环可在1ms内检测陈旧度；三层权限模型在五种攻击场景中全部防御成功。五个正确性实验确认零未授权交付、零不变式违反，并实现了现有平台未提供的审批通道隔离

Conclusion: Context Kubernetes成功解决了企业知识编排的治理挑战，其架构比容器编排更复杂但也更有价值，现有主要平台均未提供架构级的审批通道隔离

Abstract: We introduce Context Kubernetes, an architecture for orchestrating enterprise knowledge in agentic AI systems, with a prototype implementation and eight experiments. The core observation is that delivering the right knowledge, to the right agent, with the right permissions, at the right freshness -- across an entire organization -- is structurally analogous to the container orchestration problem Kubernetes solved a decade ago. We formalize six core abstractions, a YAML-based declarative manifest for knowledge-architecture-as-code, a reconciliation loop, and a three-tier agent permission model where agent authority is always a strict subset of human authority. Three value experiments show: (1) without governance, agents serve phantom content from deleted sources and leak cross-domain data in 26.5% of queries; (2) without freshness monitoring, stale content is served silently -- with reconciliation, staleness is detected in under 1ms; (3) in five attack scenarios, flat permissions block 0/5 attacks, basic RBAC blocks 4/5, and the three-tier model blocks 5/5. Five correctness experiments confirm zero unauthorized deliveries, zero invariant violations, and architectural enforcement of out-of-band approval isolation that no surveyed enterprise platform provides. A survey of four major platforms (Microsoft, Salesforce, AWS, Google) documents that none architecturally isolates agent approval channels. We identify four properties that make context orchestration harder than container orchestration, and argue that these make the solution more valuable.

</details>


### [233] [RationalRewards: Reasoning Rewards Scale Visual Generation Both Training and Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11626)
*Haozhe Wang,Cong Wei,Weiming Ren,Jiaming Liu,Fangzhen Lin,Wenhu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RationalRewards模型，通过结构化理性批评而非单一评分来改进视觉生成奖励模型，在训练时提供细粒度奖励，在测试时通过生成-批评-精炼循环优化输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成奖励模型将丰富的人类判断简化为单一不可解释的分数，丢弃了偏好的推理过程。需要将奖励模型从被动评估器转变为主动优化工具。

Method: 提出PARROT框架：通过锚定生成、一致性过滤和蒸馏，从现有偏好数据中恢复高质量理性批评。训练RationalRewards模型（8B参数），使其在评分前生成明确的多维度批评。

Result: RationalRewards在开源奖励模型中达到最先进的偏好预测性能，与Gemini-2.5-Pro竞争，但训练数据量少10-20倍。作为RL奖励，在文本到图像和图像编辑任务上优于标量替代方案。测试时的批评-精炼循环在多个基准上匹配或超过基于RL的微调。

Conclusion: 结构化理性批评可以将奖励模型从被动评估器转变为主动优化工具，解锁现有生成器中因次优提示而未能激发的潜在能力，为视觉生成提供更有效的训练和推理方法。

Abstract: Most reward models for visual generation reduce rich human judgments to a single unexplained score, discarding the reasoning that underlies preference. We show that teaching reward models to produce explicit, multi-dimensional critiques before scoring transforms them from passive evaluators into active optimization tools, improving generators in two complementary ways: at training time, structured rationales provide interpretable, fine-grained rewards for reinforcement learning; at test time, a Generate-Critique-Refine loop turns critiques into targeted prompt revisions that improve outputs without any parameter updates. To train such a reward model without costly rationale annotations, we introduce Preference-Anchored Rationalization (PARROT), a principled framework that recovers high-quality rationales from readily available preference data through anchored generation, consistency filtering, and distillation. The resulting model, RationalRewards (8B), achieves state-of-the-art preference prediction among open-source reward models, competitive with Gemini-2.5-Pro, while using 10-20x less training data than comparable baselines. As an RL reward, it consistently improves text-to-image and image-editing generators beyond scalar alternatives. Most strikingly, its test-time critique-and-refine loop matches or exceeds RL-based fine-tuning on several benchmarks, suggesting that structured reasoning can unlock latent capabilities in existing generators that suboptimal prompts fail to elicit.

</details>


### [234] [Why Do Large Language Models Generate Harmful Content?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11663)
*Rajesh Ganguli,Raha Moraffah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于因果中介分析的方法，用于识别大语言模型生成有害内容的因果因素，通过多层次分析发现有害生成主要发生在模型后层，由MLP模块而非注意力模块的故障引起，并与作为有害生成门控机制的神经元相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已被证明会生成有害内容，但此类行为背后的根本原因尚未得到充分探索。研究者希望深入理解有害内容生成的因果机制，为模型安全性和可控性提供理论基础。

Method: 采用因果中介分析方法，在模型层、模块（MLP和注意力块）以及单个神经元三个粒度上进行多层次分析。该方法系统性地识别导致有害生成的具体因果因素。

Result: 实验表明：1）有害生成主要发生在模型的后层；2）主要由MLP模块而非注意力模块的故障引起；3）与作为有害生成门控机制的稀疏神经元集合相关。早期层用于理解提示中的有害性上下文，该信号通过模型传播，在后层和MLP块中产生有害性信号，最终传递到最后层的稀疏神经元集，这些神经元据此决定是否生成有害内容。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLM有害内容生成的因果机制：早期层进行有害性上下文理解，信号通过模型传播，MLP模块在后层产生有害性信号，最终由稀疏神经元集作为门控机制决定有害内容生成。这为针对性干预和模型安全改进提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to generate harmful content. However, the underlying causes of such behavior remain under explored. We propose a causal mediation analysis-based approach to identify the causal factors responsible for harmful generation. Our method performs a multi-granular analysis across model layers, modules (MLP and attention blocks), and individual neurons. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art LLMs indicate that harmful generation arises in the later layers of the model, results primarily from failures in MLP blocks rather than attention blocks, and is associated with neurons that act as a gating mechanism for harmful generation. The results indicate that the early layers in the model are used for a contextual understanding of harmfulness in a prompt, which is then propagated through the model, to generate harmfulness in the late layers, as well as a signal indicating harmfulness through MLP blocks. This is then further propagated to the last layer of the model, specifically to a sparse set of neurons, which receives the signal and determines the generation of harmful content accordingly.

</details>


### [235] [DreamKG: A KG-Augmented Conversational System for People Experiencing Homelessness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11703)
*Javad M Alizadeh,Genhui Zheng,Chiu C Tan,Yuzhou Chen,Omar Martinez,Philip McCallion,Ying Ding,Chenguang Yang,AnneMarie Tomosky,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DreamKG是一个基于知识图谱增强的对话系统，专门为无家可归者提供准确、及时的社区服务信息，通过结合Neo4j知识图谱和结构化查询理解，有效解决LLM幻觉问题，在位置感知和时间敏感查询方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无家可归者在获取社区服务信息时面临严重障碍，现有大型语言模型容易产生幻觉，无法提供准确、及时的服务信息。需要一种可靠系统来提供经过验证的、最新的组织、服务、位置和营业时间信息。

Method: 结合Neo4j知识图谱与结构化查询理解的混合架构，系统能够进行空间推理（基于距离的推荐）和时间过滤（营业时间查询），将LLM的灵活性与知识图谱的可靠性相结合。

Result: 初步评估显示，在相关查询上比Google Search AI优越59%，能够拒绝84%的不相关查询。系统能够可靠处理位置感知和时间敏感查询，为弱势群体有效改善服务可及性。

Conclusion: DreamKG展示了混合架构的潜力，通过结合LLM的灵活性和知识图谱的可靠性，能够为弱势群体提供更准确、可靠的服务信息访问途径，有效解决信息获取障碍问题。

Abstract: People experiencing homelessness (PEH) face substantial barriers to accessing timely, accurate information about community services. DreamKG addresses this through a knowledge graph-augmented conversational system that grounds responses in verified, up-to-date data about Philadelphia organizations, services, locations, and hours. Unlike standard large language models (LLMs) prone to hallucinations, DreamKG combines Neo4j knowledge graphs with structured query understanding to handle location-aware and time-sensitive queries reliably. The system performs spatial reasoning for distance-based recommendations and temporal filtering for operating hours. Preliminary evaluation shows 59% superiority over Google Search AI on relevant queries and 84% rejection of irrelevant queries. This demonstration highlights the potential of hybrid architectures that combines LLM flexibility with knowledge graph reliability to improve service accessibility for vulnerable populations effectively.

</details>


### [236] [Collaborative Multi-Agent Scripts Generation for Enhancing Imperfect-Information Reasoning in Murder Mystery Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11741)
*Keyang Zhong,Junlin Xie,Hefeng Wu,Haofeng Li,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出协作多智能体框架，通过角色驱动的剧本生成和两阶段训练策略，提升视觉语言模型在多人游戏（如谋杀之谜）中的多跳推理和欺骗抗性能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在感知任务中表现出色，但在多人游戏场景下面对不完美和欺骗性信息时，复杂多跳推理能力会下降。谋杀之谜游戏作为典型代表，需要基于不同意图角色提供的部分线索推断隐藏真相，这暴露了现有模型的局限性。

Method: 1) 提出协作多智能体框架，用于评估和合成高质量角色驱动的多人游戏剧本，生成包含角色背景、视觉/文本线索、多跳推理链的丰富多模态上下文；2) 设计两阶段智能体监控训练策略：a) 基于思维链的微调，使用包含不确定性和欺骗建模的策划与合成数据集；b) 基于GRPO的强化学习，通过智能体监控的奖励塑形，鼓励模型发展角色特定推理行为和有效的多模态多跳推理。

Result: 广泛实验表明，该方法显著提升了视觉语言模型在叙事推理、隐藏事实提取和欺骗抗性理解方面的性能。模型在不确定、对抗性和社会复杂条件下的推理能力得到明显增强。

Conclusion: 该方法为在不完美信息、对抗性和社会复杂条件下训练和评估视觉语言模型提供了可扩展的解决方案，为未来多模态多跳推理基准测试奠定了基础，推动了模型在复杂社交推理场景中的发展。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities in perceptual tasks, yet they degrade in complex multi-hop reasoning under multiplayer game settings with imperfect and deceptive information. In this paper, we study a representative multiplayer task, Murder Mystery Games, which require inferring hidden truths based on partial clues provided by roles with different intentions. To address this challenge, we propose a collaborative multi-agent framework for evaluating and synthesizing high-quality, role-driven multiplayer game scripts, enabling fine-grained interaction patterns tailored to character identities (i.e., murderer vs. innocent). Our system generates rich multimodal contexts, including character backstories, visual and textual clues, and multi-hop reasoning chains, through coordinated agent interactions. We design a two-stage agent-monitored training strategy to enhance the reasoning ability of VLMs: (1) chain-of-thought based fine-tuning on curated and synthetic datasets that model uncertainty and deception; (2) GRPO-based reinforcement learning with agent-monitored reward shaping, encouraging the model to develop character-specific reasoning behaviors and effective multimodal multi-hop inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly boosts the performance of VLMs in narrative reasoning, hidden fact extraction, and deception-resilient understanding. Our contributions offer a scalable solution for training and evaluating VLMs under uncertain, adversarial, and socially complex conditions, laying the groundwork for future benchmarks in multimodal multi-hop reasoning under imperfect information.

</details>


### [237] [Retrieval Is Not Enough: Why Organizational AI Needs Epistemic Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11759)
*Federico Bottino,Carlo Ferrero,Nicholas Dosio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OIDA框架通过结构化知识对象和知识引力引擎提升组织AI的认知保真度，引入QUESTION机制建模组织无知，在有限token预算下实现接近完整上下文的认知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前组织AI系统缺乏认知结构，无法区分已采纳决策与废弃假设、争议主张与确定结论、已知事实与未解决问题。组织AI的瓶颈不是检索保真度而是认知保真度——系统表示承诺强度、矛盾状态和组织无知作为可计算属性的能力。

Method: 提出OIDA框架：1) 将组织知识结构化为带类型的知识对象，包含认知类别、重要性分数（带类别特定衰减）和有符号矛盾边；2) 知识引力引擎确定性维护分数，具有收敛保证（最大度数<7时收敛，经验上可容忍至43度）；3) 引入QUESTION作为建模无知的原语，具有逆衰减特性，能随时间增加紧迫性地浮现组织未知内容。

Result: 1) 在受控比较中（n=10响应对），OIDA的RAG条件（3,868 tokens）获得EQS 0.530，而完整上下文基线（108,687 tokens）为0.848，28.1倍token预算差异是主要混杂因素；2) QUESTION机制统计验证显著（Fisher p=0.0325，OR=21.0）；3) 建立了形式化属性，决定性消融实验（E4）已预注册但尚未运行。

Conclusion: OIDA通过结构化知识对象、知识引力引擎和QUESTION机制，为组织AI提供了认知保真度框架，能够在有限资源下有效表示承诺强度、矛盾状态和组织无知，显著提升知识管理质量。

Abstract: Organizational knowledge used by AI agents typically lacks epistemic structure: retrieval systems surface semantically relevant content without distinguishing binding decisions from abandoned hypotheses, contested claims from settled ones, or known facts from unresolved questions. We argue that the ceiling on organizational AI is not retrieval fidelity but \emph{epistemic} fidelity--the system's ability to represent commitment strength, contradiction status, and organizational ignorance as computable properties.
  We present OIDA, a framework that structures organizational knowledge as typed Knowledge Objects carrying epistemic class, importance scores with class-specific decay, and signed contradiction edges. The Knowledge Gravity Engine maintains scores deterministically with proved convergence guarantees (sufficient condition: max degree $< 7$; empirically robust to degree 43). OIDA introduces QUESTION-as-modeled-ignorance: a primitive with inverse decay that surfaces what an organization does \emph{not} know with increasing urgency--a mechanism absent from all surveyed systems. We describe the Epistemic Quality Score (EQS), a five-component evaluation methodology with explicit circularity analysis. In a controlled comparison ($n{=}10$ response pairs), OIDA's RAG condition (3,868 tokens) achieves EQS 0.530 vs.\ 0.848 for a full-context baseline (108,687 tokens); the $28.1\times$ token budget difference is the primary confound. The QUESTION mechanism is statistically validated (Fisher $p{=}0.0325$, OR$=21.0$). The formal properties are established; the decisive ablation at equal token budget (E4) is pre-registered and not yet run.

</details>


### [238] [GenTac: Generative Modeling and Forecasting of Soccer Tactics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11786)
*Jiayuan Rao,Tianlin Gui,Haoning Wu,Yanfeng Wang,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenTac是一个基于扩散模型的生成框架，用于建模足球比赛中的开放战术，通过连续轨迹和离散事件生成多样化的长期未来轨迹，支持丰富的上下文条件控制，并能泛化到其他团队运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算模型通常产生单一确定性轨迹预测或专注于高度结构化的定位球，无法捕捉真实比赛演化的固有方差和分支可能性，因此需要一种能够建模足球战术随机性和多智能体特性的新方法。

Method: 提出GenTac扩散生成框架，将足球战术建模为连续多球员轨迹和离散语义事件的随机过程。从历史跟踪数据中学习球员移动的底层分布，支持丰富的上下文条件控制（对手行为、球队/联赛风格、战略目标），并将连续空间动态映射到15类战术事件空间。

Result: 在TacBench基准测试中展示四个关键能力：1) 高几何精度同时保持团队结构一致性；2) 准确模拟风格细微差别，区分特定球队和联赛；3) 支持可控反事实模拟，基于攻防指导改变空间控制和预期威胁指标；4) 可靠预测未来战术结果。成功泛化到篮球、美式足球和冰球等其他动态团队运动。

Conclusion: GenTac为开放战术建模提供了一种有效的生成框架，能够捕捉足球比赛的内在随机性和多样性，支持丰富的条件控制，并展示了跨运动泛化的潜力，为团队运动的战术分析和模拟提供了新工具。

Abstract: Modeling open-play soccer tactics is a formidable challenge due to the stochastic, multi-agent nature of the game. Existing computational approaches typically produce single, deterministic trajectory forecasts or focus on highly structured set-pieces, fundamentally failing to capture the inherent variance and branching possibilities of real-world match evolution. Here, we introduce GenTac, a diffusion-based generative framework that conceptualizes soccer tactics as a stochastic process over continuous multi-player trajectories and discrete semantic events. By learning the underlying distribution of player movements from historical tracking data, GenTac samples diverse, plausible, long-horizon future trajectories. The framework supports rich contextual conditioning, including opponent behavior, specific team or league playing styles, and strategic objectives, while grounding continuous spatial dynamics into a 15-class tactical event space. Extensive evaluations on our proposed benchmark, TacBench, demonstrate four key capabilities: (1) GenTac achieves high geometric accuracy while strictly preserving the collective structural consistency of the team; (2) it accurately simulates stylistic nuances, distinguishing between specific teams (e.g., Auckland FC) and leagues (e.g., A-League versus German leagues); (3) it enables controllable counterfactual simulations, demonstrably altering spatial control and expected threat metrics based on offensive or defensive guidance; and (4) it reliably anticipates future tactical outcomes directly from generated rollouts. Finally, we demonstrate that GenTac can be successfully trained to generalize to other dynamic team sports, including basketball, American football, and ice hockey.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [HyEm: Query-Adaptive Hyperbolic Retrieval for Biomedical Ontologies via Euclidean Vector Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09550)
*Ou Deng,Shoji Nishimura,Atsushi Ogihara,Qun Jin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HyEm是一个轻量级检索层，将双曲本体嵌入集成到现有欧几里得ANN基础设施中，通过半径控制的双曲嵌入、标准欧几里得数据库存储和精确双曲重排序，自适应结合语义相似性和层次距离，在保持实体中心查询性能的同时提升层次导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学知识检索增强生成面临层次感知本体基础挑战：HPO、DO、MeSH等资源使用深度"is-a"分类法，但生产栈依赖欧几里得嵌入和ANN索引。双曲嵌入适合层次表示，但面临两个障碍：(1)缺乏原生向量数据库支持，(2)在层次无关的实体中心查询中可能表现不佳。

Method: HyEm学习半径控制的双曲嵌入，将原点对数映射向量存储在标准欧几里得数据库中进行候选检索，然后应用精确双曲重排序。查询自适应门输出连续混合权重，在重排序时结合欧几里得语义相似性和双曲层次距离。通过半径约束下的双Lipschitz分析为ANN过采样和维度提供实践指导。

Result: 在生物医学本体子集上的实验表明，HyEm在实体中心查询中保持了94-98%的欧几里得基线性能，同时在层次导航和混合意图查询中显著改进，在适度过采样下保持可索引性。

Conclusion: HyEm成功将双曲本体嵌入集成到现有欧几里得ANN基础设施中，通过自适应混合机制平衡语义相似性和层次距离，解决了双曲嵌入在实际部署中的两大障碍，为生物医学知识检索提供了有效的层次感知解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for biomedical knowledge faces a hierarchy-aware ontology grounding challenge: resources like HPO, DO, and MeSH use deep ``is-a" taxonomies, yet production stacks rely on Euclidean embeddings and ANN indexes. While hyperbolic embeddings suit hierarchical representation, they face two barriers: (i) lack of native vector database support, and (ii) risk of underperforming on entity-centric queries where hierarchy is irrelevant. We present HyEm, a lightweight retrieval layer integrating hyperbolic ontology embeddings into existing Euclidean ANN infrastructure. HyEm learns radius-controlled hyperbolic embeddings, stores origin log-mapped vectors in standard Euclidean databases for candidate retrieval, then applies exact hyperbolic reranking. A query-adaptive gate outputs continuous mixing weights, combining Euclidean semantic similarity with hyperbolic hierarchy distance at reranking time. Our bi-Lipschitz analysis under radius constraints provides practical guidance for ANN oversampling and dimensionality.Experiments on biomedical ontology subsets demonstrate HyEm preserves 94-98% of Euclidean baseline performance on entity-centric queries while substantially improving hierarchy-navigation and mixed-intent queries, maintaining indexability at moderate oversampling.

</details>


### [240] [SemaCDR: LLM-Powered Transferable Semantics for Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09551)
*Chunxu Zhang,Shanqiang Huang,Zijian Zhang,Jiahong Liu,Linsong Yu,Ruiqi Wan,Bo Yang,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SemaCDR：基于大语言模型的语义驱动跨域序列推荐框架，通过构建统一语义空间解决传统方法依赖领域特定特征、缺乏可迁移性的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域推荐方法通常依赖领域特定的特征或标识符，这些特征缺乏跨域可迁移性，限制了捕捉跨域语义模式的能力。需要一种能够构建统一语义空间的方法来解决数据稀疏性和冷启动问题。

Method: 提出SemaCDR框架：1）利用大语言模型生成领域无关语义，与领域特定内容结合创建多视图项目特征；2）通过对比正则化对齐特征；3）系统生成领域特定和领域无关语义，采用自适应融合生成统一偏好表示；4）使用自适应融合机制对齐跨域行为序列，综合源域、目标域和混合域的交互序列。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，SemaCDR持续优于最先进的基线方法，证明了其在捕捉连贯的域内模式同时促进跨域知识转移方面的有效性。

Conclusion: SemaCDR通过大语言模型构建统一语义空间，有效解决了跨域推荐中的领域特定特征可迁移性限制问题，能够同时捕捉域内模式和促进跨域知识转移，为跨域序列推荐提供了有效的语义驱动解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) addresses the data sparsity and cold-start problems in the target domain by leveraging knowledge from data-rich source domains. However, existing CDR methods often rely on domain-specific features or identifiers that lack transferability across different domains, limiting their ability to capture inter-domain semantic patterns. To overcome this, we propose SemaCDR, a semantics-driven framework for cross-domain sequential recommendation that leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct a unified semantic space. SemaCDR creates multiview item features by integrating LLM-generated domain-agnostic semantics with domain-specific content, aligned by contrastive regularization. SemaCDR systematically creates LLM-generated domain-specific and domain-agnostic semantics, and employs adaptive fusion to generate unified preference representations. Furthermore, it aligns cross-domain behavior sequences with an adaptive fusion mechanism to synthesize interaction sequences from source, target, and mixed domains. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that SemaCDR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness in capturing coherent intra-domain patterns while facilitating knowledge transfer across domains.

</details>


### [241] [MCERF: Advancing Multimodal LLM Evaluation of Engineering Documentation with Enhanced Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09552)
*Kiarash Naghavi Khanghah,Hoang Anh Nguyen,Anna C. Doris,Amir Mohammad Vahedi,Daniele Grandi,Faez Ahmed,Hongyi Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MCERF框架，通过多模态检索器结合大语言模型推理，提升工程文档问答准确率41.1%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程规范和技术标准包含密集文本、表格和插图等多模态信息，传统基于纯文本的RAG系统难以有效处理这些复杂内容，需要开发更先进的多模态检索增强生成系统

Method: 建立MCERF框架，采用ColPali多模态检索器结合四种检索推理策略：混合查找模式、视觉到文本融合、高推理LLM模式和自一致性决策；设计两种路由方法：单案例路由和多智能体系统

Result: 在DesignQA基准测试中，系统在所有任务上平均准确率相对提升41.1%，在多模态和推理密集型任务上表现显著改善，无需完整规则书摄入

Conclusion: 视觉语言检索、模块化推理和自适应路由的结合能够实现工程用例中可扩展的文档理解，该模块化框架设计为未来多模态系统提供了可重用模板

Abstract: Engineering rulebooks and technical standards contain multimodal information like dense text, tables, and illustrations that are challenging for retrieval augmented generation (RAG) systems. Building upon the DesignQA framework [1], which relied on full-text ingestion and text-based retrieval, this work establishes a Multimodal ColPali Enhanced Retrieval and Reasoning Framework (MCERF), a system that couples a multimodal retriever with large language model reasoning for accurate and efficient question answering from engineering documents. The system employs the ColPali, which retrieves both textual and visual information, and multiple retrieval and reasoning strategies: (i) Hybrid Lookup mode for explicit rule mentions, (ii) Vision to Text fusion for figure and table guided queries, (iii) High Reasoning LLM mode for complex multi modal questions, and (iv) SelfConsistency decision to stabilize responses. The modular framework design provides a reusable template for future multimodal systems regardless of underlying model architecture. Furthermore, this work establishes and compares two routing approaches: a single case routing approach and a multi-agent system, both of which dynamically allocate queries to optimal pipelines. Evaluation on the DesignQA benchmark illustrates that this system improves average accuracy across all tasks with a relative gain of +41.1% from baseline RAG best results, which is a significant improvement in multimodal and reasoning-intensive tasks without complete rulebook ingestion. This shows how vision language retrieval, modular reasoning, and adaptive routing enable scalable document comprehension in engineering use cases.

</details>


### [242] [Decoding Ancient Oracle Bone Script via Generative Dictionary Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09668)
*Yin Wu,Gangjian Zhang,Jiayu Chen,Chang Xu,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于深度学习与字形演化原理的甲骨文破译新方法，将破译任务从分类重构为字典检索，通过生成全面的甲骨文变体合成字典，显著提升了未识别字符的破译准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲骨文作为中国商代的重要文字系统，目前约4600个字符中仅有1500个被破译，大量3000年前的铭文仍未被完全理解。现有计算方法在未识别字符上的准确率不足3%，存在数据稀缺和算法黑箱问题，亟需新的破译方法。

Method: 将破译任务从分类重构为基于字典的检索方法。利用深度学习结合字形演化原理，为现代汉字生成全面的甲骨文变体合成字典。学者可查询未知铭文，检索视觉相似的候选字符，提供透明证据而非算法黑箱。

Result: 该方法在未识别字符上取得了显著突破：Top-10准确率达到54.3%，Top-50准确率达到86.6%。相比现有方法不足3%的准确率有巨大提升，为甲骨文破译提供了可扩展、透明的框架。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种可推广的AI辅助考古发现方法学，通过深度学习与字形演化原理的结合，显著加速了甲骨文这一关键未破译文字系统的解读进程，为其他古代文字的破译提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Understanding humanity's earliest writing systems is crucial for reconstructing civilization's origins, yet many ancient scripts remain undeciphered. Oracle Bone Script (OBS) from China's Shang dynasty exemplifies this challenge: only approximately 1,500 of roughly 4,600 characters have been decoded, and a substantial portion of these 3,000-year-old inscriptions remains only partially understood. Limited by extreme data scarcity, existing computational methods achieve under 3% accuracy on unseen characters -- the core palaeographic challenge. We overcome this by reframing decipherment from classification to dictionary-based retrieval. Using deep learning guided by character evolution principles, we generate a comprehensive synthetic dictionary of plausible OBS variants for modern Chinese characters. Scholars query unknown inscriptions to retrieve visually similar candidates with transparent evidence, replacing algorithmic black boxes with interpretable hypotheses. Our approach achieves 54.3% Top-10 and 86.6% Top-50 accuracy for unseen characters. This scalable, transparent framework accelerates decipherment of a pivotal undeciphered script and establishes a generalizable methodology for AI-assisted archaeological discovery.

</details>


### [243] [Evaluating Scene-based In-Situ Item Labeling for Immersive Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09698)
*Jiazhou Liang,Yifan Simon Liu,David Guo,Minqi Sun,Yilun Jiang,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了沉浸式对话推荐系统（ICRS）的概念，并针对其中的物品标签选择问题建立了新的评估框架，揭示了现有方法的三大局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩展现实（XR）技术的普及，对话推荐系统正朝着视觉沉浸式体验发展。在沉浸式CRS中，推荐物品直接在用户的场景视觉环境中高亮显示并辅以现场标签。然而，如何选择和评估哪些信息应作为沉浸式标签呈现仍然是一个开放性问题。

Method: 作者将信息需求分为显式意图满足和主动信息需求两个类别，并基于此定义了新的物品标签选择评估指标。在三个数据集（时尚、电影推荐、零售购物）和ICRS场景中，对基于信息检索（IR）、大语言模型（LLM）和视觉语言模型（VLM）的方法进行了基准测试。

Result: 评估揭示了现有方法的三个重要局限性：1）未能利用场景特定的信息模态（如时尚的视觉线索、零售的元数据）；2）呈现了视觉上可推断的冗余信息；3）仅从显式对话中难以准确预测用户的主动信息需求。

Conclusion: 这项工作为ICRS中的现场物品标签选择提供了新的评估范式，并指出了未来研究的关键挑战，包括需要更好地整合多模态信息和预测用户潜在需求。

Abstract: The growing ubiquity of Extended Reality (XR) is driving Conversational Recommendation Systems (CRS) toward visually immersive experiences. We formalize this paradigm as Immersive CRS (ICRS), where recommended items are highlighted directly in the user's scene-based visual environment and augmented with in-situ labels. While item recommendation has been widely studied, the problem of how to select and evaluate which information to present as immersive labels remains an open problem. To this end, we introduce a principled categorization of information needs into explicit intent satisfaction and proactive information needs and use these to define novel evaluation metrics for item label selection. We benchmark IR-, LLM-, and VLM-based methods across three datasets and ICRS scenarios: fashion, movie recommendation, and retail shopping. Our evaluation reveals three important limitations of existing methods: (1) they fail to leverage scenario-specific information modalities (e.g., visual cues for fashion, meta-data for retail), (2) they present redundant information that is visually inferable, and (3) they poorly anticipate users' proactive information needs from explicit dialogue alone. In summary, this work provides both a novel evaluation paradigm for in-situ item labeling in ICRS and highlights key challenges for future work.

</details>


### [244] [Jamendo-MT-QA: A Benchmark for Multi-Track Comparative Music Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09721)
*Junyoung Koh,Jaeyun Lee,Soo Yong Kim,Gyu Hyeong Choi,Jung In Koh,Jordan Phillips,Yeonjin Lee,Min Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Jamendo-MT-QA数据集，用于多音轨比较问答任务，包含36,519个比较QA项，覆盖12,173个音轨对，支持三种问题类型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐问答研究主要关注单音轨理解，但听众常以比较方式描述音乐，现有基准缺乏对多音轨推理的系统性评估

Method: 基于Jamendo-QA数据集，从Jamendo的Creative Commons授权音轨构建多音轨比较问答数据集；使用LLM辅助流水线生成和过滤比较问题；采用自动指标和LLM-as-a-Judge评估音频-语言模型

Result: 构建了Jamendo-MT-QA数据集，包含36,519个比较QA项，覆盖12,173个音轨对，每个音轨对生成三种问题类型：是/否问题、简短答案问题和句子级问题

Conclusion: 提出了首个多音轨比较问答数据集和基准，填补了音乐理解中比较推理评估的空白，为音频-语言模型在多音轨场景下的性能评估提供了新工具

Abstract: Recent work on music question answering (Music-QA) has primarily focused on single-track understanding, where models answer questions about an individual audio clip using its tags, captions, or metadata. However, listeners often describe music in comparative terms, and existing benchmarks do not systematically evaluate reasoning across multiple tracks. Building on the Jamendo-QA dataset, we introduce Jamendo-MT-QA, a dataset and benchmark for multi-track comparative question answering. From Creative Commons-licensed tracks on Jamendo, we construct 36,519 comparative QA items over 12,173 track pairs, with each pair yielding three question types: yes/no, short-answer, and sentence-level questions. We describe an LLM-assisted pipeline for generating and filtering comparative questions, and benchmark representative audio-language models using both automatic metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation.

</details>


### [245] [A Mathematical Theory of Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09733)
*Yin Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于成对比较而非绝对分数的排名系统数学理论，通过成对边际分解分析因子级贡献，建立了从局部影响到全局结构的统一框架，并扩展到非线性评分和几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排名系统通常基于标量分数产生有序列表，但排名本身实际上只依赖于成对比较。现有方法过于关注绝对分数而忽视了排名的成对比较本质，需要建立以成对边际为中心的理论框架来更准确地分析因子对排名的影响。

Method: 1. 线性情况：将成对边际精确分解为因子级贡献，证明L1局部影响份额是纯因子细化的唯一一致预算规则；2. 非线性情况：通过局部线性化和成对积分梯度扩展框架，证明因子级路径归因的路径独立性条件；3. 几何框架：通过排列空间、分数空间超平面交叉、离散精确性和三角形旋度、Hodge类诊断、根空间/Weyl室几何等概念组织分析。

Result: 1. 建立了成对边际分解的数学理论，证明了L1局部影响份额的唯一性；2. 在log-绝对权重坐标中，全局影响结构是凸势的梯度，其Jacobian是竞争图拉普拉斯算子；3. 证明了影响交换满足有限能量恒等式和零交换刚性定律；4. 建立了交互曲率定理，确定了因子级路径归因路径独立的条件；5. 提供了从线性到非线性、从局部到全局的统一分析框架。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一个以成对比较为中心的排名系统分析框架，通过成对边际分解提供了因子影响的精确量化方法。该框架统一了线性与非线性情况，建立了影响结构的几何解释，为排名系统的可解释性分析提供了理论基础。理论表明，只有在加性机制下才能实现完全的因子级唯一性，而交叉因子交互会导致路径依赖性。

Abstract: Ranking systems produce ordered lists from scalar scores, yet the ranking itself depends only on pairwise comparisons. We develop a mathematical theory that takes this observation seriously, centering the analysis on pairwise margins rather than absolute scores.
  In the linear case, each pairwise margin decomposes exactly into factor-level contributions. We prove that the resulting L_1 local influence share is the unique budgeting rule consistent with pure factor refinement. Aggregating local shares yields a global influence structure: in log-absolute-weight coordinates, this structure is the gradient of a convex potential, its Jacobian is a competition-graph Laplacian, and Influence Exchange -- the reallocation of pairwise control across model states -- satisfies a finite energy identity with a zero-exchange rigidity law.
  For nonlinear scoring, the pairwise margin remains well-defined, but factor-level decomposition becomes path-dependent due to cross-factor interactions. We prove an interaction-curvature theorem: factorwise path attribution is path-independent if and only if the relevant mixed partial derivatives vanish, recovering full factorwise uniqueness exactly in the additive regime. The framework extends through local linearization and Pairwise Integrated Gradients.
  The geometric arc continues through permutation space, score-space hyperplane crossings, discrete exactness and triangle curl, Hodge-like diagnostics, and root-space/Weyl-chamber geometry -- organized as successive interpretive closures of the same pairwise-first analytical progression.

</details>


### [246] [Exploring Structural Complexity in Normative RAG with Graph-based approaches: A case study on the ETSI Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09868)
*Aiman Al Masoud,Marco Arazzi,Simone Germani,Antonino Nocera*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究了针对工业标准和规范性文档的图RAG架构，通过将文档结构信息嵌入检索流程来提升检索性能，相比传统向量检索能更好地捕捉标准文档的层次结构和关系特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业标准和规范性文档具有复杂的层次结构、领域特定词汇和大量交叉引用依赖，这使得大型语言模型难以直接处理。虽然检索增强生成（RAG）为LLM微调提供了计算高效的替代方案，但标准的向量检索可能无法捕捉规范性文档中固有的潜在结构和关系特征。

Method: 提出专门针对标准和监管文档独特结构和词汇特征的RAG方法，重点研究图RAG架构。评估了多种轻量级、低延迟策略，将文档结构直接嵌入检索工作流程。使用ETSI EN 301 489系列等公共标准作为研究对象，通过自定义合成的问答数据集进行定量性能分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，将结构和词汇信息纳入索引可以在一定程度上增强检索性能，为自动化的规范和标准制定提供了可扩展的框架。

Conclusion: 图RAG架构通过将信息表示为互连节点，从简单的语义相似性转向更稳健的关系感知检索机制，能够更好地处理标准和规范性文档的复杂特征，为解决当前缺乏技术标准最优索引策略的实证证据提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Industrial standards and normative documents exhibit intricate hierarchical structures, domain-specific lexicons, and extensive cross-referential dependencies, which making it challenging to process them directly by Large Language Models (LLMs).
  While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) provides a computationally efficient alternative to LLM fine-tuning, standard "vanilla" vector-based retrieval may fail to capture the latent structural and relational features intrinsic in normative documents.
  With the objective of shedding light on the most promising technique for building high-performance RAG solutions for normative, standards, and regulatory documents, this paper investigates the efficacy of Graph RAG architectures, which represent information as interconnected nodes, thus moving from simple semantic similarity toward a more robust, relation-aware retrieval mechanism.
  Despite the promise of graph-based techniques, there is currently a lack of empirical evidence as to which is the optimal indexing strategy for technical standards. Therefore, to help solve this knowledge gap, we propose a specialized RAG methodology tailored to the unique structure and lexical characteristics of standards and regulatory documents.
  Moreover, to keep our investigation grounded, we focus on well-known public standards, such as the ETSI EN 301 489 series. We evaluate several lightweight and low-latency strategies designed to embed document structure directly into the retrieval workflow.
  The considered approaches are rigorously tested against a custom synthesized Q&A dataset, facilitating a quantitative performance analysis. Our experimental results demonstrate that the incorporation of structural and lexical information into the index can enhance, at least to some extent, retrieval performance, providing a scalable framework for automated normative and standards elaboration.

</details>


### [247] [Reproduction Beyond Benchmarks: ConstBERT and ColBERT-v2 Across Backends and Query Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09982)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Ashish David,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文发现多向量检索模型在长叙事查询上性能大幅下降，这是由MaxSim算子的架构限制导致的，无法通过数据适应或参数调整完全解决


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是验证多向量检索模型的可复现性，不仅要关注数值准确性，更要评估架构鲁棒性，特别是在处理长叙事查询时的表现

Method: 评估ColBERT-v2和ConstBERT模型在五个维度上的表现，包括消融实验分析MaxSim算子的token均匀加权机制，以及研究未记录的后端参数对稀疏质心覆盖的影响

Result: 在MS-MARCO上ConstBERT可复现到0.05% MRR@10内，但在TREC ToT 2025的长叙事查询上性能下降86-97%；性能在20词后达到平台期；未记录参数导致8点差距；3倍数据微调反而使性能下降29%

Conclusion: 多向量检索模型的架构约束无法仅通过适应方法克服，MaxSim算子的均匀token加权机制无法区分信号与填充噪声，限制了长查询处理能力

Abstract: Reproducibility must validate architectural robustness, not just numerical accuracy. We evaluate ColBERT-v2 and ConstBERT across five dimensions, finding that while ConstBERT reproduces within 0.05% MRR@10 on MS-MARCO, both models show a drop of 86-97% on long, narrative queries (TREC ToT 2025). Ablations prove this failure is architectural: performance plateaus at 20 words because the MaxSim operator's uniform token weighting cannot distinguish signal from filler noise. Furthermore, undocumented backend parameters create an 8-point gap due to ConstBERT's sparse centroid coverage, and fine-tuning with 3x more data actually degrades performance by up to 29%. We conclude that architectural constraints in multi-vector retrieval cannot be overcome by adaptation alone. Code: https://github.com/utshabkg/multi-vector-reproducibility.

</details>


### [248] [MOSAIC: Multi-Domain Orthogonal Session Adaptive Intent Capture for Prescient Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10147)
*Abderaouf Bahi,Mourad Boughaba,Ibtissem Gasmi,Warda Deghmane,Amel Ourici*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个多领域正交会话自适应意图捕获框架，通过将用户偏好分解为三个正交组件来改进多领域会话推荐系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多领域推荐方法难以区分跨领域交互与领域内交互的贡献，限制了构建丰富且可迁移的用户表示能力

Method: 采用三重编码器架构，分别处理领域特定、领域通用和跨序列排他表示，通过域掩码目标和梯度反转层的对抗训练强制正交性，使用动态门控机制调节各组件在时间步上的贡献

Result: 在两个大规模真实世界基准测试中，MOSAIC在推荐准确性上持续优于最先进基线，同时提供领域特定与跨领域偏好信号交互的可解释性洞察

Conclusion: 正交偏好分解作为下一代多领域推荐系统的原则性策略具有显著潜力，能够有效分离和建模复杂的用户意图

Abstract: Capturing user intent across heterogeneous behavioral domains stands as a fundamental challenge in session-based recommender systems. Yet, existing multi-domain approaches frequently fail to isolate the distinct contribution of cross-domain interactions from those arising within individual domains, limiting their ability to build rich and transferable user representations. In this work, we propose MOSAIC, a Multi-Domain Orthogonal Session Adaptive Intent Capture framework that explicitly factorizes user preferences into three orthogonal components: domain-specific, domain-common, and cross-sequence-exclusive representations. Our approach employs a triple-encoder architecture, where each encoder is dedicated to one preference type, enforced through domain masking objectives and adversarial training via a gradient reversal layer. Representational alignment and mutual independence constraints are jointly optimized to ensure clean preference separation. Additionally, a dynamic gating mechanism modulates the relative contribution of each component at every timestep, yielding a unified and temporally adaptive session-level user representation. We conduct extensive experiments on two large-scale real-world benchmarks spanning multiple domains and interaction types. The ablation study validates that each component domain-specific encoding, domain-common modeling, cross-sequence representation, and dynamic gating contributes meaningfully to the overall performance. Experimental results demonstrate that MOSAIC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in recommendation accuracy, while simultaneously providing interpretable insights into the interplay between domain-specific and cross-domain preference signals. These findings highlight the potential of orthogonal preference decomposition as a principled strategy for next-generation multi-domain recommender systems.

</details>


### [249] [SID-Coord: Coordinating Semantic IDs for ID-based Ranking in Short-Video Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10471)
*Guowen Li,Yuepeng Zhang,Shunyu Zhang,Yi Zhang,Xiaoze Jiang,Yi Wang,Jingwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SID-Coord是一个轻量级语义ID框架，通过将可训练的离散语义ID集成到基于ID的排序模型中，协调基于哈希ID的记忆能力和基于语义ID的泛化能力，解决短视频搜索排序中的长尾项目泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模短视频搜索排序模型通常基于哈希项目ID的稀疏共现信号进行训练，虽然能有效记忆频繁交互，但难以泛化到曝光有限的长尾项目。这种记忆与泛化的权衡是工业系统中的长期挑战。

Method: SID-Coord将语义表示为结构化标识符，在统一建模框架内协调基于HID的记忆和基于SID的泛化。包含三个核心组件：1)基于层次SID的注意力融合模块捕捉多级语义；2)目标感知的HID-SID门控机制自适应平衡记忆与泛化；3)SID驱动的兴趣对齐模块建模目标项目与用户历史的语义相似度分布。

Result: 在线A/B实验在真实生产环境中显示出统计显著的改进：搜索长播率提升+0.664%，搜索播放时长增加+0.369%。该框架无需修改现有生产排序系统的主干模型即可集成。

Conclusion: SID-Coord通过将语义信号作为结构化标识符而非辅助密集特征，有效解决了ID基排序模型中的记忆-泛化权衡问题，在保持轻量级的同时显著提升了长尾项目的推荐效果。

Abstract: Large-scale short-video search ranking models are typically trained on sparse co-occurrence signals over hashed item identifiers (HIDs). While effective at memorizing frequent interactions, such ID-based models struggle to generalize to long-tailed items with limited exposure. This memorization-generalization trade-off remains a longstanding challenge in such industrial systems. We propose SID-Coord, a lightweight Semantic ID framework that incorporates discrete, trainable semantic IDs (SIDs) directly into ID-based ranking models. Instead of treating semantic signals as auxiliary dense features, SID-Coord represents semantics as structured identifiers and coordinates HID-based memorization with SID-based generalization within a unified modeling framework. To enable effective coordination, SID-Coord introduces three components: (1) an attention-based fusion module over hierarchical SIDs to capture multi-level semantics, (2) a target-aware HID-SID gating mechanism that adaptively balances memorization and generalization, and (3) a SID-driven interest alignment module that models the semantic similarity distribution between target items and user histories. SID-Coord can be integrated into existing production ranking systems without modifying the backbone model. Online A/B experiments in a real-world production environment show statistically significant improvements, with a +0.664% gain in long-play rate in search and a +0.369% increase in search playback duration.

</details>


### [250] [Evaluating Small Open LLMs for Medical Question Answering: A Practical Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10535)
*Avi-ad Avraam Buskila*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个评估小型本地可部署LLM在医疗问答中表现的开源框架，特别强调输出可重复性作为核心指标，发现即使低温生成下模型自一致性也很低，揭示了单次评估基准忽略的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗问答中LLM的应用不仅需要高平均准确率，更需要输出一致性。在线健康社区如Reddit已成为主要医疗信息来源但易受错误信息影响，部署LLM作为助手时，输出一致性与正确性同等重要。现有评估通常忽略可重复性这一关键安全指标。

Method: 开发了一个实用的开源评估框架，将可重复性作为与词汇和语义准确性并列的一级指标。管道计算八个质量指标（包括BERTScore、ROUGE-L和LLM-as-judge评分），以及两个基于重复推理（每个问题N=10次运行）的模型内可重复性指标。评估了三个模型（Llama 3.1 8B、Gemma 3 12B、MedGemma 1.5 4B）在50个MedQuAD问题上的表现（共1,500个回答）。

Result: 尽管采用低温生成（T=0.2），模型自一致性最高仅0.20，87-97%的输出是唯一的。临床微调的MedGemma 1.5 4B在质量和可重复性上都表现不如更大的通用模型，但由于它也是最小的模型，这种比较混淆了领域微调和模型规模的影响。

Conclusion: 医疗问答中LLM的可重复性是一个被忽视但关键的安全问题，单次评估基准完全无法捕捉这一风险。提出的框架为从业者提供了可复制的模型选择工具，强调了在医疗应用中评估输出一致性的重要性。

Abstract: Incorporating large language models (LLMs) in medical question answering demands more than high average accuracy: a model that returns substantively different answers each time it is queried is not a reliable medical tool. Online health communities such as Reddit have become a primary source of medical information for millions of users, yet they remain highly susceptible to misinformation; deploying LLMs as assistants in these settings amplifies the need for output consistency alongside correctness. We present a practical, open-source evaluation framework for assessing small, locally-deployable open-weight LLMs on medical question answering, treating reproducibility as a first-class metric alongside lexical and semantic accuracy. Our pipeline computes eight quality metrics, including BERTScore, ROUGE-L, and an LLM-as-judge rubric, together with two within-model reproducibility metrics derived from repeated inference (N=10 runs per question). Evaluating three models (Llama 3.1 8B, Gemma 3 12B, MedGemma 1.5 4B) on 50 MedQuAD questions (N=1,500 total responses) reveals that despite low-temperature generation (T=0.2), self-agreement across runs reaches at most 0.20, while 87-97% of all outputs per model are unique -- a safety gap that single-pass benchmarks entirely miss. The clinically fine-tuned MedGemma 1.5 4B underperforms the larger general-purpose models on both quality and reproducibility; however, because MedGemma is also the smallest model, this comparison confounds domain fine-tuning with model scale. We describe the methodology in sufficient detail for practitioners to replicate or extend the evaluation for their own model-selection workflows. All code and data pipelines are available at https://github.com/aviad-buskila/llm_medical_reproducibility.

</details>


### [251] [NSFL: A Post-Training Neuro-Symbolic Fuzzy Logic Framework for Boolean Operators in Neural Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10604)
*Vladi Vexler,Ofer Idan,Gil Lederman,Dima Sivov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NSFL框架为密集检索器提供无需训练的神经符号模糊逻辑计算，通过神经符号增量保持原子意义，利用球面查询优化实现可扩展实时检索，在多种编码器配置上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准密集检索器缺乏处理多原子逻辑约束的本机计算能力，传统几何基线存在表示崩溃和流形逃逸问题，需要一种无需重新训练就能在神经嵌入空间中适应形式逻辑运算的框架。

Method: 提出神经符号模糊逻辑（NSFL）框架：1）基于孤立零阶相似度分数锚定逻辑运算；2）使用神经符号增量（NS-Delta）主动引导表示，这是从上下文融合推导的一阶边际差异；3）通过球面查询优化（SQO）利用黎曼优化将模糊公式投影到流形稳定查询向量中。

Result: 在六种不同编码器配置和两种模态（包括零样本和SOTA微调模型）上验证，NSFL使mAP提升高达+81%。即使应用于专门为逻辑推理微调的编码器，仍能提供平均20%、最高47%的额外提升。

Conclusion: NSFL建立了一个无需训练、顺序感知的高维空间计算框架，为未来动态扩展和学习流形逻辑奠定了基础，解决了传统几何基线的表示崩溃问题，同时保持了纯原子意义。

Abstract: Standard dense retrievers lack a native calculus for multi-atom logical constraints. We introduce Neuro-Symbolic Fuzzy Logic (NSFL), a framework that adapts formal t-norms and t-conorms to neural embedding spaces without requiring retraining. NSFL operates as a first-order hybrid calculus: it anchors logical operations on isolated zero-order similarity scores while actively steering representations using Neuro-Symbolic Deltas (NS-Delta) -- the first-order marginal differences derived from contextual fusion. This preserves pure atomic meaning while capturing domain reliance, preventing the representation collapse and manifold escape endemic to traditional geometric baselines. For scalable real-time retrieval, Spherical Query Optimization (SQO) leverages Riemannian optimization to project these fuzzy formulas into manifold-stable query vectors. Validated across six distinct encoder configurations and two modalities (including zero-shot and SOTA fine-tuned models), NSFL yields mAP improvements up to +81%. Notably, NSFL provides an additive 20% average and up to 47% boost even when applied to encoders explicitly fine-tuned for logical reasoning. By establishing a training-free, order-aware calculus for high-dimensional spaces, this framework lays the foundation for future dynamic scaling and learned manifold logic.

</details>


### [252] [From Query to Conscience: The Importance of Information Retrieval in Empowering Socially Responsible Consumerism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10751)
*Frans van der Sluis,Leif Azzopardi,Florian Meier*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文主张信息检索(IR)领域在促进社会责任消费中发挥关键作用，通过减少信息不对称、降低责任搜索成本、校准消费者知识，帮助消费者做出更道德和明智的购买决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者在在线购物时面临伦理决策的信息障碍，导致意图与行为之间的差距。虽然价格和质量是主要购买因素，但伦理考量日益影响消费者行为，而现有搜索系统在支持责任决策方面效率低下。

Method: 提出三个相互关联的视角：1) 将责任消费重构为减少信息不对称的信息提取问题；2) 将产品搜索重新定义为需要降低责任搜索成本和负担的复杂任务；3) 将搜索重新构想为帮助消费者在购买决策中弥合意识差距的知识校准过程。

Result: 通过这三个视角，为IR系统如何帮助消费者从查询到良心的转变提供了理论框架，将日常产品搜索转化为更道德和知情选择的机会。

Conclusion: 呼吁IR社区开发新颖的IR系统和界面，解决社会责任消费的复杂性，构建使伦理决策更加知情、便利且符合经济现实的技术，推动该领域在促进道德消费中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Millions of consumers search for products online each day, aiming to find items that meet their needs at an acceptable price. While price and quality are major factors in purchasing decisions, ethical considerations increasingly influence consumer behavior, giving rise to the socially responsible consumer. Insights from a recent survey of over 600 consumers reveal that many barriers to ethical shopping stem from information-seeking challenges, often leading to decisions made under uncertainty. These challenges contribute to the intention-behaviour gap, where consumers' desire to make ethical choices is undermined by limited or inaccessible information and inefficacy of search systems in supporting responsible decision-making. In this perspectives paper, we argue that the field of Information Retrieval (IR) has a critical role to play by empowering consumers to make more informed and more responsible choices. We present three interrelated perspectives: (1) reframing responsible consumption as an information extraction problem aimed at reducing information asymmetries; (2) redefining product search as a complex task requiring interfaces that lower the cost and burden of responsible search; and (3) reimagining search as a process of knowledge calibration that helps consumers bridge gaps in awareness when making purchasing decisions. Taken together, these perspectives outline a path from query to conscience, one where IR systems help transform everyday product searches into opportunities for more ethical and informed choices. We advocate for the development of new and novel IR systems and interfaces that address the intricacies of socially responsible consumerism, and call on the IR community to build technologies that make ethical decisions more informed, convenient, and aligned with economic realities.

</details>


### [253] [BDIViz in Action: Interactive Curation and Benchmarking for Schema Matching Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10763)
*Eden Wu,Christos Koutras,Cláudio T. Silva,Juliana Freire*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BDIViz是一个用于模式匹配的交互式可视化系统，支持LLM辅助验证和人类在环的基准测试与迭代匹配器开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模式匹配是数据集成的基础，但现有评估方法受限于基准测试多样性不足和缺乏交互式验证框架，难以有效评估和比较不同的匹配方法。

Method: BDIViz是一个交互式可视化系统，采用热图展示自动匹配结果，支持层次导航、缩放和过滤。用户可直接在热图中验证匹配，并通过协调视图检查属性描述、示例值和分布。LLM助手为选定的候选匹配生成结构化解释。系统提供标准化接口集成新匹配器，用户验证结果作为演化的真实数据用于实时性能评估。

Result: 系统支持两种互补场景：1) 数据协调：用户将大型表格数据集映射到目标模式，进行值级检查和LLM生成的解释；2) 开发者在环基准测试：开发者集成自定义匹配器，观察性能指标并优化算法。

Conclusion: BDIViz扩展版本解决了数据集成研究中的关键需求：人类在环的基准测试和迭代匹配器开发，能够基准测试新算法、通过专家验证构建高质量真实数据集，以及跨不同模式和领域比较匹配器行为。

Abstract: Schema matching remains fundamental to data integration, yet evaluating and comparing matching methods is hindered by limited benchmark diversity and lack of interactive validation frameworks. BDIViz, recently published at IEEE VIS 2025, is an interactive visualization system for schema matching with LLM-assisted validation. Given source and target datasets, BDIViz applies automatic matching methods and visualizes candidates in an interactive heatmap with hierarchical navigation, zoom, and filtering. Users validate matches directly in the heatmap and inspect ambiguous cases using coordinated views that show attribute descriptions, example values, and distributions. An LLM assistant generates structured explanations for selected candidates to support decision-making. This demonstration showcases a new extension to BDIViz that addresses a critical need in data integration research: human-in-the-loop benchmarking and iterative matcher development. New matchers can be integrated through a standardized interface, while user validations become evolving ground truth for real-time performance evaluation. This enables benchmarking new algorithms, constructing high-quality ground-truth datasets through expert validation, and comparing matcher behavior across diverse schemas and domains. We demonstrate two complementary scenarios: (i) data harmonization, where users map a large tabular dataset to a target schema with value-level inspection and LLM-generated explanations; and (ii) developer-in-the-loop benchmarking, where developers integrate custom matchers, observe performance metrics, and refine their algorithms.

</details>


### [254] [CMedTEB & CARE: Benchmarking and Enabling Efficient Chinese Medical Retrieval via Asymmetric Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10937)
*Angqing Jiang,Jianlyu Chen,Zhe Fang,Yongcan Wang,Xinpeng Li,Keyu Ding,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了中文医学文本嵌入基准CMedTEB和不对称检索器CARE，通过轻量级查询编码器和强大文档编码器的组合，在保持低延迟的同时提升医学文本检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学文本检索面临两个主要问题：1）基于LLM的嵌入模型虽然检索能力强，但延迟高、计算成本大，难以应用于实时场景；2）缺乏全面且高质量的中文医学文本检索基准，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 1）构建CMedTEB基准：通过多LLM投票管道并由临床专家验证，涵盖检索、重排序和语义文本相似性三种任务；2）提出CARE不对称检索器：采用轻量级BERT风格编码器处理在线查询，使用强大的LLM编码器处理离线文档；3）设计两阶段训练策略：渐进式桥接查询和文档表示。

Result: CARE在CMedTEB基准上超越了最先进的对称模型，实现了更优的检索性能，同时没有增加推理延迟。实验验证了不对称架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量的CMedTEB基准和创新的CARE不对称检索器，解决了医学文本检索中准确性与延迟的权衡问题，为中文医学信息检索提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Effective medical text retrieval requires both high accuracy and low latency. While LLM-based embedding models possess powerful retrieval capabilities, their prohibitive latency and high computational cost limit their application in real-time scenarios. Furthermore, the lack of comprehensive and high-fidelity benchmarks hinders progress in Chinese medical text retrieval. In this work, we introduce the Chinese Medical Text Embedding Benchmark (CMedTEB), a benchmark spanning three kinds of practical embedding tasks: retrieval, reranking, and semantic textual similarity (STS). Distinct from purely automated datasets, CMedTEB is curated via a rigorous multi-LLM voting pipeline validated by clinical experts, ensuring gold-standard label quality while effectively mitigating annotation noise. On this foundation, we propose the Chinese Medical Asymmetric REtriever (CARE), an asymmetric architecture that pairs a lightweight BERT-style encoder for online query encoding with a powerful LLM-based encoder for offline document encoding. However, optimizing such an asymmetric retriever with two structurally different encoders presents distinctive challenges. To address this, we introduce a novel two-stage training strategy that progressively bridges the query and document representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CARE surpasses state-of-the-art symmetric models on CMedTEB, achieving superior retrieval performance without increasing inference latency.

</details>


### [255] [Multi-Faceted Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding for Semantic-Aware Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10947)
*Jing Qi,Yuxiang Wang,Zhiyuan Yu,Xiaoliang Xu,Yuanshi Zheng,Tianxing Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MF-CKGE提出了一种多面持续知识图谱嵌入框架，通过分离新旧知识到不同嵌入空间并采用语义解耦，解决了传统CKGE方法中实体多面语义动态演化的表示问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识图谱嵌入方法通过正则化或重放旧知识来缓解灾难性遗忘，但它们在同一个嵌入空间中混合新旧知识，无法捕捉实体随时间动态演化的多面语义，导致跨时间快照的链接预测精度下降。

Method: MF-CKGE采用多面嵌入框架：离线学习阶段，将时间上的新旧知识分离到不同嵌入空间以避免知识纠缠，并采用语义解耦减少语义冗余；在线推理阶段，通过量化语义重要性自适应识别与查询语义相关的实体嵌入，减少查询无关噪声的干扰。

Result: 在八个数据集上的实验表明，MF-CKGE在MRR和Hits@10指标上分别比最佳基线平均提升1.7%和1.4%，最大提升分别达到2.7%和3.8%。

Conclusion: MF-CKGE通过分离新旧知识嵌入空间和语义解耦，有效解决了持续知识图谱嵌入中实体多面语义动态演化的表示问题，显著提升了跨时间快照的链接预测性能。

Abstract: Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding (CKGE) aims to continually learn embeddings for new knowledge, i.e., entities and relations, while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Most existing CKGE methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting via regularization or replaying old knowledge. They conflate new and old knowledge of an entity within the same embedding space to seek a balance between them. However, entities inherently exhibit multi-faceted semantics that evolve dynamically as their relational contexts change over time. A shared embedding fails to capture and distinguish these temporal semantic variations, degrading lifelong link prediction accuracy across snapshots. To address this, we propose a Multi-Faceted CKGE framework (MF-CKGE) for semantic-aware link prediction. During offline learning, MF-CKGE separates temporal old and new knowledge into distinct embedding spaces to prevent knowledge entanglement and employs semantic decoupling to reduce semantic redundancy, thereby improving space efficiency. During online inference, MF-CKGE adaptively identifies semantically query-relevant entity embeddings by quantifying their semantic importance, reducing interference from query-irrelevant noise. Experiments on eight datasets show that MF-CKGE achieves an average (maximum) improvement of 1.7% (2.7%) and 1.4% (3.8%) in MRR and Hits@10, respectively, over the best baseline. Our source code and datasets are available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MF-CKGE-04E5.

</details>


### [256] [ARHN: Answer-Centric Relabeling of Hard Negatives with Open-Source LLMs for Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11092)
*Hyewon Choi,Jooyoung Choi,Hansol Jang,Hyun Kim,Chulmin Yun,ChangWook Jun,Stanley Jungkyu Choi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ARHN是一个两阶段框架，利用开源LLM通过答案中心的相关性信号来精炼硬负样本，通过重新标记假负样本和过滤模糊负样本来改善检索模型的训练监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬负样本挖掘在实践中会引入假负样本和模糊负样本（包含相关或部分答案的段落），这种标签噪声会导致不一致的监督并降低检索效果。

Method: ARHN采用两阶段框架：第一阶段，对每个查询-段落对，提示LLM生成基于段落的答案片段或指示段落不支持答案；第二阶段，应用基于LLM的列表排序，根据对查询的直接可回答性对候选段落排序。将排名高于原始正样本的段落重新标记为额外正样本，在排名低于正样本的段落中，排除包含答案片段的段落以避免模糊监督。

Result: 在BEIR基准测试中评估了三种配置：仅重新标记、仅过滤、以及两者组合。组合策略在所有数据集上一致优于单独步骤，表明联合重新标记假负样本和过滤模糊负样本能为神经检索模型训练提供更干净的监督。

Conclusion: ARHN通过严格依赖开源模型，建立了一个成本效益高且可扩展的精炼流程，适用于大规模训练，能有效改善检索模型的训练监督质量。

Abstract: Neural retrievers are often trained on large-scale triplet data comprising a query, a positive passage, and a set of hard negatives. In practice, hard-negative mining can introduce false negatives and other ambiguous negatives, including passages that are relevant or contain partial answers to the query. Such label noise yields inconsistent supervision and can degrade retrieval effectiveness.
  We propose ARHN (Answer-centric Relabeling of Hard Negatives), a two-stage framework that leverages open-source LLMs to refine hard negative samples using answer-centric relevance signals. In the first stage, for each query-passage pair, ARHN prompts the LLM to generate a passage-grounded answer snippet or to indicate that the passage does not support an answer. In the second stage, ARHN applies an LLM-based listwise ranking over the candidate set to order passages by direct answerability to the query. Passages ranked above the original positive are relabeled to additional positives. Among passages ranked below the positive, ARHN excludes any that contain an answer snippet from the negative set to avoid ambiguous supervision.
  We evaluated ARHN on the BEIR benchmark under three configurations: relabeling only, filtering only, and their combination. Across datasets, the combined strategy consistently improves over either step in isolation, indicating that jointly relabeling false negatives and filtering ambiguous negatives yields cleaner supervision for training neural retrieval models. By relying strictly on open-source models, ARHN establishes a cost-effective and scalable refinement pipeline suitable for large-scale training.

</details>


### [257] [R3-VAE: Reference Vector-Guided Rating Residual Quantization VAE for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11440)
*Qiang Wan,Ze Yang,Dawei Yang,Ying Fan,Xin Yan,Siyang Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出R3-VAE框架解决生成式推荐中语义标识符生成的两大挑战：训练不稳定性和评估低效性，通过参考向量、点积评分机制和两个评估指标实现性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐中的语义标识符生成面临两大挑战：1) 训练不稳定（直通估计器梯度传播不足和初始化敏感）；2) 评估效率低（依赖昂贵的生成式推荐训练和A/B测试）

Method: 提出R3-VAE框架，包含三个关键创新：1) 参考向量作为语义锚点缓解初始化敏感；2) 基于点积的评分机制稳定训练防止码书崩溃；3) 两个SID评估指标（语义凝聚性和偏好区分度）作为正则项

Result: 在六个基准测试中优于SOTA方法，在三个Amazon数据集上Recall@10平均提升14.2%，NDCG@10提升15.5%；在新闻推荐平台A/B测试中MRR提升1.62%，StayTime/U提升0.83%；替换CTR模型item ID后内容冷启动提升15.36%

Conclusion: R3-VAE有效解决了语义标识符生成的训练不稳定和评估低效问题，在工业规模推荐场景中展现出强大的适用性和商业价值

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has gained traction for its merits of superior performance and cold-start capability. As the vital role in GR, Semantic Identifiers (SIDs) represent item semantics through discrete tokens. However, current techniques for SID generation based on vector quantization face two main challenges: (i) training instability, stemming from insufficient gradient propagation through the straight-through estimator and sensitivity to initialization; and (ii) inefficient SID quality assessment, where industrial practice still depends on costly GR training and A/B testing. To address these challenges, we propose Reference Vector-Guided Rating Residual Quantization VAE (R3-VAE). This framework incorporates three key innovations: (i) a reference vector that functions as a semantic anchor for the initial features, thereby mitigating sensitivity to initialization; (ii) a dot product-based rating mechanism designed to stabilize the training process and prevent codebook collapse; and (iii) two SID evaluation metrics, Semantic Cohesion and Preference Discrimination, serving as regularization terms during training. Empirical results on six benchmarks demonstrate that R3-VAE outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving an average improvement of 14.2% in Recall@10 and 15.5% in NDCG@10 across three Amazon datasets. Furthermore, we perform GR training and online A/B tests on a prominent news recommendation platform. Our method achieves a 1.62% improvement in MRR and a 0.83% gain in StayTime/U versus baselines. Additionally, we employ R3-VAE to replace the item ID of CTR model, resulting in significant improvements in content cold start by 15.36%, corroborating the strong applicability and business value in industry-scale recommendation scenarios.

</details>


### [258] [EA-Agent: A Structured Multi-Step Reasoning Agent for Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11686)
*Yixuan Nan,Xixun Lin,Yanmin Shang,Ge Zhang,Zheng Fang,Fang Fang,Yanan Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EA-Agent：一种基于推理驱动的实体对齐智能体，通过多步规划和执行将实体对齐构建为结构化推理过程，提高可解释性并降低计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实体对齐方法依赖知识表示学习，在噪声或稀疏监督场景下性能受限；现有LLM方法将大语言模型视为黑盒决策器，缺乏可解释性且直接使用大规模三元组导致推理成本过高

Method: 提出EA-Agent推理驱动智能体，将实体对齐构建为结构化推理过程，包含多步规划和执行；引入属性和关系三元组选择器，在输入LLM前过滤冗余三元组以提高效率

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，EA-Agent持续优于现有实体对齐方法，达到最先进的性能水平

Conclusion: EA-Agent通过推理驱动方法解决了现有LLM实体对齐方法的可解释性和效率问题，为知识图谱融合提供了更优的解决方案

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) aims to identify entities across different knowledge graphs (KGs) that refer to the same real-world object and plays a critical role in knowledge fusion and integration. Traditional EA methods mainly rely on knowledge representation learning, but their performance is often limited under noisy or sparsely supervised scenarios. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been introduced to EA and achieved notable improvements by leveraging rich semantic knowledge. However, existing LLM-based EA approaches typically treat LLMs as black-box decision makers, resulting in limited interpretability, and the direct use of large-scale triples substantially increases inference cost. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{EA-Agent}, a reasoning-driven agent for EA. EA-Agent formulates EA as a structured reasoning process with multi-step planning and execution, enabling interpretable alignment decisions. Within this process, it introduces attribute and relation triple selectors to filter redundant triples before feeding them into the LLM, effectively addressing efficiency challenges. Experimental results on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that EA-Agent consistently outperforms existing EA methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance. The source code is available at https://github.com/YXNan0110/EA-Agent.

</details>


### [259] [Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models for Evidence-Informed Guidance on Cannabidiol Use in Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09548)
*Ali Abedi,Charlene H. Chu,Shehroz S. Khan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个检索增强的大型语言模型框架，结合结构化提示工程和精选的大麻二酚证据，为老年人（包括认知障碍者）提供情境感知指导，并提出自动化评估框架验证模型安全性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人常经历疼痛和睡眠障碍等慢性症状，可能考虑使用大麻二酚进行症状管理。然而，安全使用需要适当剂量、仔细滴定和药物相互作用意识，但污名化和有限的健康素养往往限制了理解。基于大型语言模型和检索增强生成的对话式人工智能系统可能支持大麻二酚教育，但其安全性和可靠性尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 研究开发了检索增强的大型语言模型框架，结合结构化提示工程与精选的大麻二酚证据，为老年人（包括认知障碍者）生成情境感知指导。提出了自动化、无需标注的评估框架，在没有标准化基准的情况下对领先的独立模型和检索增强模型进行基准测试。通过变化症状、偏好、认知状态、人口统计学、合并症、药物、大麻使用史和护理支持等参数，生成了64个多样化用户场景。评估了多个最先进的模型，包括集成多个检索系统的新型集成检索架构。

Result: 在三种自动化评估策略中，检索增强模型始终比独立模型产生更谨慎、更符合指南的建议，其中集成方法表现最佳。这些发现表明结构化检索提高了AI驱动的大麻二酚教育的可靠性和安全性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了结构化检索能够提高AI驱动的大麻二酚教育的可靠性和安全性，并为评估敏感健康背景下使用的AI工具提供了一个可复现的框架。

Abstract: Older adults commonly experience chronic conditions such as pain and sleep disturbances and may consider cannabidiol for symptom management. Safe use requires appropriate dosing, careful titration, and awareness of drug interactions, yet stigma and limited health literacy often limit understanding. Conversational artificial intelligence systems based on large language models and retrieval-augmented generation may support cannabidiol education, but their safety and reliability remain insufficiently evaluated. This study developed a retrieval-augmented large language model framework that combines structured prompt engineering with curated cannabidiol evidence to generate context-aware guidance for older adults, including those with cognitive impairment. We also proposed an automated, annotation-free evaluation framework to benchmark leading standalone and retrieval-augmented models in the absence of standardized benchmarks. Sixty-four diverse user scenarios were generated by varying symptoms, preferences, cognitive status, demographics, comorbidities, medications, cannabis history, and caregiver support. Multiple state-of-the-art models were evaluated, including a novel ensemble retrieval architecture that integrates multiple retrieval systems. Across three automated evaluation strategies, retrieval-augmented models consistently produced more cautious and guideline-aligned recommendations than standalone models, with the ensemble approach performing best. These findings demonstrate that structured retrieval improves the reliability and safety of AI-driven cannabidiol education and provide a reproducible framework for evaluating AI tools used in sensitive health contexts.
